````python
from pmcp.types import (McpTaskInfo, RemoteMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig,
                        ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum)

sent_context: dict[str, Any] = {}


def _kw(m: ClientManager, context: Any, conn: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """The tree's `_record_task` keywords: rev 5 requires `connection`."""
    params = inspect.signature(m._record_task).parameters
    kw: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if "requestor_context" in params:
        kw["requestor_context"] = context
    if "fallback_context" in params:
        kw["fallback_context"] = None
    if "connection" in params:
        kw["connection"] = conn
    return kw


def build(trigger: str, cap: int = 2, context: Any = None) -> tuple[ClientManager, list[str]]:
    m = ClientManager()
    if hasattr(m._tasks, "per_server"):
        m._tasks.per_server = cap
    m._clients["tasks"] = ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name="tasks", source="custom",
            config=RemoteMcpServerConfig(type="streamable-http", url="https://t.example/mcp")),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=MagicMock(),
        status=ServerStatus(name="tasks", status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=1,
                            server_capabilities={"tasks": {}}))
    for t in ("b", "a"):
        m._record_task("tasks", McpTaskInfo(task_id=t, status="working"), **_kw(m, context, m._clients["tasks"]))
    cancels: list[str] = []
    sent_context.clear()
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        if method == "tasks/list":
            return {"tasks": [{"taskId": "new", "status": "working"}]}
        cancels.append(params["taskId"])
        sent_context[params["taskId"]] = (params.get("task") or {}).get("requestorContext")
        if trigger == "re-tracked":
            if params["taskId"] == "a":
                for listed in ("x", "b"):
                    m._record_task("tasks", McpTaskInfo(task_id=listed, status="working"), **_kw(m, None, m._clients["tasks"]))
            return {"taskId": params["taskId"], "status": "cancelled"}
        if trigger == "concurrent-record":
            await m.list_tasks("tasks")
            return {"taskId": params["taskId"], "status": "cancelled"}
        return {"taskId": "other-" + params["taskId"], "status": "working"}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    return m, cancels

async def main() -> None:
    for trigger in ("concurrent-record", "foreign-task-id"):
        m, cancels = build(trigger)
        print(f"  {trigger}: disconnect_server(force=True) -> {await m.disconnect_server('tasks', force=True)}; tasks/cancel sent for {cancels}")
        m, cancels = build(trigger)
        print(f"  {trigger}: cancel_active_tasks() -> {await m.cancel_active_tasks()}; tasks/cancel sent for {cancels}")
    # Round-3 F001: b evicted mid-teardown, then re-tracked by a concurrent
    # listing that records no requestor_context. Which context does b's cancel carry?
    m, cancels = build("re-tracked", context={"tenant": "t-1"})
    print(f"  re-tracked: cancel_active_tasks() -> {await m.cancel_active_tasks()}; tasks/cancel sent for {cancels}; b's context {sent_context.get('b')}")
    # Not F001: b is evicted *before* the teardown starts (cap 1), so no
    # snapshot holds it. The residual the cap itself brings, on any tree.
    m, cancels = build("concurrent-record", cap=1)
    print(f"  evicted-before: tracked {sorted(t for _, t in m._tasks)}; disconnect_server(force=True) -> {await m.disconnect_server('tasks', force=True)}; tasks/cancel sent for {cancels}")

asyncio.run(main())
````

### Script — `codex_f001.py` (the round-4 codex finding; `PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src python codex_f001.py`)

````python
"""Round-4 codex F001 on a given tree: while `a`'s teardown cancel awaits, a
reconnect replaces `beta`'s connection (possibly another tenant). What does
the replacement receive? Keyword arguments adapt to the tree's signature."""
import asyncio, inspect
from typing import Any
from pmcp.client.manager import ClientManager, ManagedClient
from pmcp.types import (McpTaskInfo, RemoteMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig,
                        ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum)

def client(name: str, url: str) -> ManagedClient:
    return ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name=name, source="custom",
            config=RemoteMcpServerConfig(type="streamable-http", url=url)),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=object(),
        status=ServerStatus(name=name, status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=0,
                            server_capabilities={"tasks": {}}))

async def main() -> None:
    m = ClientManager()
    first, old = client("alpha", "https://alpha.example/mcp"), client("beta", "https://old-tenant.example/mcp")
    replacement = client("beta", "https://new-tenant.example/mcp")
    m._clients.update(alpha=first, beta=old)
    params = inspect.signature(m._record_task).parameters
    def rec(server: str, task: str, ctx: Any, conn: ManagedClient) -> None:
        kw: dict[str, Any] = {"requestor_context": ctx}
        if "fallback_context" in params:
            kw["fallback_context"] = None
        if "connection" in params:
            kw["connection"] = conn
        m._record_task(server, McpTaskInfo(task_id=task, status="working"), **kw)
    rec("alpha", "a", None, first)
    rec("beta", "b", {"tenant": "old-tenant"}, old)
    to_replacement: list[Any] = []
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, p: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        if managed is first:
            await m._cleanup_client("beta", old)
            m._clients["beta"] = replacement
        if managed is replacement:
            to_replacement.append((method, p))
        return {"taskId": p["taskId"], "status": "cancelled"}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    print(f"  cancel_active_tasks() -> {await m.cancel_active_tasks()}; sent to the replacement: {to_replacement}")

asyncio.run(main())
````

### Script — `readback.py` (the round-5 finding; `PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src python readback.py`)

````python
"""Round-5 F001 on a given tree: a call runs on connection A; while it is in
flight the server is replaced by B, which tracks its own task "t"
("from-B"). A's reply names "t" ("from-A"). What does the caller get?"""
import asyncio, inspect
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from pmcp.client.manager import ClientManager, ManagedClient
from pmcp.policy.policy import PolicyManager
from pmcp.tools.handlers import GatewayTools
from pmcp.types import (McpTaskInfo, RemoteMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig,
                        RiskHint, ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum, ToolInfo)

def conn() -> ManagedClient:
    return ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name="tasks", source="custom",
            config=RemoteMcpServerConfig(type="streamable-http", url="https://t.example/mcp")),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=MagicMock(),
        status=ServerStatus(name="tasks", status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=1,
                            server_capabilities={"tasks": {}}))

def record(m: ClientManager, info: McpTaskInfo, c: ManagedClient) -> None:
    params = inspect.signature(m._record_task).parameters
    kw: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if "requestor_context" in params: kw["requestor_context"] = None
    if "fallback_context" in params: kw["fallback_context"] = None
    if "connection" in params: kw["connection"] = c
    m._record_task("tasks", info, **kw)

def manager() -> ClientManager:
    m = ClientManager()
    m._tools["tasks::run"] = ToolInfo(tool_id="tasks::run", server_name="tasks", tool_name="run",
        description="d", short_description="d", input_schema={"type": "object"}, tags=[],
        risk_hint=RiskHint.LOW, execution={"taskSupport": "optional"})
    m._clients["tasks"] = conn()
    return m

def replace_with_b(m: ClientManager) -> None:
    m._remove_server_indexes("tasks")
    b = conn(); m._clients["tasks"] = b
    record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="t", status="working", status_message="from-B"), b)

async def main() -> None:
    m = manager()
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        replace_with_b(m)
        return {"task": {"taskId": "t", "status": "completed", "statusMessage": "from-A"}}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    out = await GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager()).invoke({"tool_id": "tasks::run", "arguments": {}, "task": {}})
    print(f"  gateway.invoke: task.status_message={out.task.status_message if out.task else None!r}")

    m = manager(); record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="t", status="working"), m._clients["tasks"])
    async def send2(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        replace_with_b(m)
        return {"result": {"v": "A"}, "task": {"taskId": "t", "status": "completed", "statusMessage": "from-A"}}
    m._send_request = send2  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    out2 = await GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager()).tasks_result({"server_name": "tasks", "task_id": "t"})
    print(f"  gateway.tasks_result: task={out2.task.status_message if out2.task else None!r} result={out2.result!r}")

    m = manager()
    async def send3(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        replace_with_b(m)
        return {"tasks": [{"taskId": "t", "status": "working", "statusMessage": "from-A"}]}
    m._send_request = send3  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    out3 = await GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager()).tasks_list({"server_name": "tasks"})
    print(f"  gateway.tasks_list: {[t.status_message for t in out3.tasks]!r}")

asyncio.run(main())
````

### Script — `isolation.py` (the round-6 findings; `PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src python isolation.py`)

````python
"""Round-6 codex F001/F002 on a given tree (PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src).
F001: while A's tasks/get awaits, B replaces A and records its own "t"
(created_at 222, tool tasks::B, tenant B); A replies created_at 111.
F002: "t" evicted; while A's tasks/result awaits, B replaces A; A's reply has
no task, so the tasks/get fallback runs."""
import asyncio, inspect
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from pmcp.client.manager import ClientManager, ManagedClient
from pmcp.policy.policy import PolicyManager
from pmcp.tools.handlers import GatewayTools
from pmcp.types import (McpTaskInfo, RemoteMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig,
                        ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum)

def conn(url: str) -> ManagedClient:
    return ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name="tasks", source="custom",
            config=RemoteMcpServerConfig(type="streamable-http", url=url)),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=MagicMock(),
        status=ServerStatus(name="tasks", status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=1,
                            server_capabilities={"tasks": {}}))

def record(m: ClientManager, info: McpTaskInfo, c: ManagedClient, **kw: Any) -> Any:
    params = inspect.signature(m._record_task).parameters
    if "requestor_context" in params: kw.setdefault("requestor_context", None)
    else: kw.pop("requestor_context", None) if kw.get("requestor_context") is None else None
    if "fallback_context" in params: kw.setdefault("fallback_context", None)
    if "connection" in params: kw["connection"] = c
    return m._record_task("tasks", info, **kw)

async def f001() -> None:
    m = ClientManager(); a, b = conn("https://a/mcp"), conn("https://b/mcp"); m._clients["tasks"] = a
    record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="t", status="working"), a)
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        m._remove_server_indexes("tasks"); m._clients["tasks"] = b
        record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="t", status="working", created_at=222), b,
               tool_id="tasks::B", requestor_context={"tenant": "B"})
        return {"taskId": "t", "status": "completed", "createdAt": 111}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    r = await m.get_task("tasks", "t")
    kept = m.get_task_record("tasks", "t")
    print(f"  F001 A's reply: (created_at, tool_id, context) = ({r.created_at}, {getattr(r, 'tool_id', None)!r}, {getattr(r, 'requestor_context', None)!r}); registry keeps B's: {kept is not None and kept.created_at == 222}")

async def f002() -> None:
    m = ClientManager(); a, b = conn("https://a/mcp"), conn("https://b/mcp"); m._clients["tasks"] = a
    if hasattr(m._tasks, "per_server"):
        m._tasks.per_server = 1
        record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="t", status="working"), a, requestor_context={"tenant": "A"})
        record(m, McpTaskInfo(task_id="evictor", status="working"), a, requestor_context={"tenant": "A"})
    to_b: list[Any] = []
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        if managed is a:
            m._remove_server_indexes("tasks"); m._clients["tasks"] = b
            return {"result": {"value": "from-A"}}
        to_b.append(method)
        return {"taskId": "t", "status": "completed", "statusMessage": "from-B"}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    try:
        out = await GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager()).tasks_result(
            {"server_name": "tasks", "task_id": "t", "requestor_context": {"tenant": "A"}})
        print(f"  F002 requests to B: {to_b}; output task: {out.task.status_message if out.task else None!r}; result: {out.result!r}; ok={out.ok}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  F002 raised {type(e).__name__}: {e}; requests to B: {to_b}")

asyncio.run(f001()); asyncio.run(f002())
````

### Script — `context_race.py` (the round-7 finding; `PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src python context_race.py`)

````python
"""Round-7 codex F001 and round-8 claude F001 on a given tree
(PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src): an older request captures OLD and awaits; while it is
in flight a newer call on the same connection supplies NEW for the same task,
with the cap evicting before the newer call (round 7), after it (round 8), or
not at all. Which context does the next cancel send?"""
import asyncio, inspect
from typing import Any
from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from pmcp.client.manager import ClientManager, ManagedClient
from pmcp.types import (McpTaskInfo, RemoteMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig,
                        RiskHint, ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum, ToolInfo)
OLD, NEW = {"correlation": "old"}, {"correlation": "new"}

def manager() -> ClientManager:
    m = ClientManager()
    m._tools["tasks::run"] = ToolInfo(tool_id="tasks::run", server_name="tasks", tool_name="run",
        description="d", short_description="d", input_schema={"type": "object"}, tags=[],
        risk_hint=RiskHint.LOW, execution={"taskSupport": "optional"})
    m._clients["tasks"] = ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name="tasks", source="custom",
            config=RemoteMcpServerConfig(type="streamable-http", url="https://t/mcp")),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=MagicMock(),
        status=ServerStatus(name="tasks", status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=1,
                            server_capabilities={"tasks": {}}))
    return m

def record(m: ClientManager, task_id: str) -> None:
    params = inspect.signature(m._record_task).parameters
    kw: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for name in ("requestor_context", "fallback_context"):
        if name in params: kw[name] = None
    if "connection" in params: kw["connection"] = m._clients["tasks"]
    m._record_task("tasks", McpTaskInfo(task_id=task_id, status="working"), **kw)

async def run(op: str, evict: str) -> Any:
    m = manager()
    if evict != "none" and hasattr(m._tasks, "per_server"):
        m._tasks.per_server = 2
    fired = False; cancels: list[Any] = []
    async def send(managed: Any, method: str, params: Any, **_: Any) -> Any:
        nonlocal fired
        if method == "tools/call":
            return {"task": {"taskId": "t", "status": "working"}}
        if method == "tasks/cancel" and fired:
            cancels.append(params["task"]["requestorContext"])
            return {"taskId": "t", "status": "cancelled"}
        if not fired:
            fired = True
            if evict == "before-newer" and hasattr(m._tasks, "per_server"):
                record(m, "x"); record(m, "y")
            await m.call_tool("tasks::run", {}, task={"requestor_context": NEW})
            if evict == "after-newer" and hasattr(m._tasks, "per_server"):
                record(m, "x"); record(m, "y")
        return {"taskId": "t", "status": "working"} if method != "tasks/result" else {"task": {"taskId": "t", "status": "working"}}
    m._send_request = send  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    await m.call_tool("tasks::run", {}, task={"requestor_context": OLD})
    if op == "tasks/get": await m.get_task("tasks", "t")
    elif op == "tasks/result": await m.get_task_result("tasks", "t")
    elif op == "tasks/cancel": await m.cancel_task("tasks", "t")
    await m.cancel_task("tasks", "t")
    return cancels

async def main() -> None:
    for evict in ("none", "before-newer", "after-newer"):
        for op in ("tasks/get", "tasks/result", "tasks/cancel"):
            print(f"  {op:12} eviction={evict:12}: next cancel sends {await run(op, evict)}")

asyncio.run(main())
````

### Script — `refresh_ignored.py` (the refresh corollary; `PYTHONPATH=<tree>/src`)

````python
"""F001 corollary: gateway.refresh ignores a failed forced disconnect."""
import asyncio, sys

from unittest.mock import MagicMock
from pmcp.client.manager import ClientManager, ManagedClient
from pmcp.policy.policy import PolicyManager
from pmcp.tools.handlers import GatewayTools
from pmcp.types import LocalMcpServerConfig, ResolvedServerConfig, ServerStatus, ServerStatusEnum

async def main() -> None:
    m = ClientManager()
    m._clients["gone"] = ManagedClient(
        config=ResolvedServerConfig(name="gone", source="custom", config=LocalMcpServerConfig(command="x")),
        is_remote=True, write_stream=MagicMock(),
        status=ServerStatus(name="gone", status=ServerStatusEnum.ONLINE, tool_count=0))
    calls = []
    async def failing(name, force=False):
        calls.append((name, force))
        return (False, 0, "Error closing transport")
    m.disconnect_server = failing  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    out = await GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager()).refresh({"force": True})
    print("disconnect calls:", calls)
    print("refresh ok:", out.ok, "errors:", out.errors, "'gone' still in _clients:", "gone" in m._clients)

asyncio.run(main())
````

### Script — `nan_relay.py` (CHANGELOG correction 1; imports a helper from the new test module, so it needs the patched tree)

````python
"""CHANGELOG correction 1: where a downstream NaN reaches the caller. Real
ClientManager + GatewayTools; the gateway's own encoder (server.py:
json.dumps(result.model_dump(), indent=2))."""
import asyncio, json, sys
sys.path.insert(0, ".")
from pmcp.policy.policy import PolicyManager
from pmcp.tools.handlers import GatewayTools
from tests.test_task_tracking_cap import _manager

NAN = float("nan")

async def main() -> None:
    m, r = _manager("tasks")
    r[("tasks", "tools/call")] = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": "x"}], "structuredContent": {"score": NAN}}
    r[("tasks", "tasks/list")] = {"tasks": [{"taskId": "t", "status": "working", "meta": {"p": NAN}}]}
    r[("tasks", "tasks/get")] = {"taskId": "t", "status": "working", "meta": {"p": NAN}}
    r[("tasks", "tasks/result")] = {"result": {"score": NAN}, "task": {"taskId": "t", "status": "completed", "meta": {"p": NAN}}}
    gt = GatewayTools(client_manager=m, policy_manager=PolicyManager())
    for name, coro in (
        ("gateway.invoke (tool result)", gt.invoke({"tool_id": "tasks::run", "arguments": {}})),
        ("gateway.tasks_list (task raw)", gt.tasks_list({"server_name": "tasks"})),
        ("gateway.tasks_get (task raw)", gt.tasks_get({"server_name": "tasks", "task_id": "t"})),
        ("gateway.tasks_result (result + task raw)", gt.tasks_result({"server_name": "tasks", "task_id": "t"})),
    ):
        out = await coro
        text = json.dumps(out.model_dump(), indent=2)
        print(f"{name}: ok={out.ok} NaN-token-in-output={'NaN' in text}")

asyncio.run(main())
````

````python
]
TESTS = ["tests/test_task_tracking_cap.py", "tests/test_task_numeric_bounds.py", "tests/test_client_manager.py", "tests/test_tools.py", "tests/test_phase4_e2e.py", "tests/test_integration.py", "tests/test_lazy_start.py"]
killed = 0
for name, rel, old, new in M:
    path = ROOT / rel
    saved = path.read_text()
    pairs = old if isinstance(old, list) else [(old, new)]
    bad = [saved.count(o) for o, _ in pairs if saved.count(o) != 1]
    if bad:
        print(f"{name}: PATTERN COUNT {bad[0]}"); continue
    mutated = saved
    for o, n in pairs:
        mutated = mutated.replace(o, n)
    path.write_text(mutated)
    try:
        r = subprocess.run([str(ROOT/".venv/bin/python"), "-m", "pytest", *TESTS, "-x", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider",
                            f"--basetemp=/var/tmp/pmcp-338-bt-viperjuice/mut", "-p", "no:randomly"],
                           cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
    finally:
        path.write_text(saved)
        # verify the restore byte for byte (the workspace volume has truncated
        # writes): stop the whole run if it did not land
        if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != hashlib.sha256(saved.encode()).hexdigest():
            print(f"{name}: RESTORE FAILED for {rel}", flush=True); sys.exit(2)
    # A kill is a test failing (round-11 nit): pytest exit code 1 with a
    # FAILED or ERROR line naming a test. A collection error (2), an internal
    # error (3), a usage error (4) or a crash counts as NOT killed.
    last = [l for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith(("FAILED tests/", "ERROR tests/"))]
    ok = r.returncode == 1 and bool(last)
    status = "KILLED" if ok else f"NOT KILLED (exit {r.returncode})"
    killed += ok
    print(f"{name}: {status} by {last[0] if last else r.stdout.splitlines()[-1:]}", flush=True)
print(f"{killed}/{len(M)} killed")
````
